3417 — kneeling one arm reach roll lift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3417 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: quadruped.

A kneeling drill where one arm reaches, rolls and lifts to open the upper back and shoulders.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a hands-and-knees setup with the torso level and the source's documented spine position.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start on hands and knees with hands under shoulders. 3417.json#/instructions/0
  2. Reach one arm forward along the floor. 3417.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll that arm up and across, rotating your chest. 3417.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lift it toward the ceiling, following your hand with your eyes. 3417.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return and repeat, then switch arms. 3417.json#/instructions/4

3418 — l-pull-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3418 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: hanging.

A pull-up with the legs held out straight in an L shape. It trains the lats, biceps and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Grab the pull-up bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. Hang with your arms fully extended and your body straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3418.json#/instructions/0, 3418.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grab the pull-up bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. Hang with your arms fully extended and your body straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3418.json#/instructions/0, 3418.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Keep the feet clear of the lower support surface and the legs quiet in a comfortable alignment; preserve source-defined knee bends or leg raises.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3418.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Pull-up/inverted-row bar. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3418.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Grab the pull-up bar with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 3418.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hang with your arms fully extended and your body straight. 3418.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your lats and biceps to pull your body up towards the bar, keeping your elbows close to your body. 3418.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue pulling until your chin is above the bar. 3418.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your body back down to the starting position. 3418.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3418.json#/instructions/5

3419 — l-sit on floor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3419 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated hold on the floor, hands beside the hips, with the legs lifted straight out. It trains the abs, hip flexors and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the floor with your legs extended in front of you. Place your hands on the floor beside your hips, fingers pointing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3419.json#/instructions/0, 3419.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place your hands on the floor beside your hips, fingers pointing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3419.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on the floor with your legs extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3419.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3419.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor with your legs extended in front of you. 3419.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands on the floor beside your hips, fingers pointing forward. 3419.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and lift your legs off the ground, keeping them straight. 3419.json#/instructions/2
  4. Try to bring your legs parallel to the floor, forming an 'L' shape with your body. 3419.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold this position for as long as you can. 3419.json#/instructions/4
  6. Slowly lower your legs back down to the starting position. 3419.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3419.json#/instructions/6

3420 — v-sit on floor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3420 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated hold with both the legs and the upper body lifted into a V shape. It trains the abs, hip flexors and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the floor with your legs extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3420.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor with your legs extended in front of you. 3420.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean back slightly and lift your legs off the ground, keeping them straight. 3420.json#/instructions/1
  3. Simultaneously, lift your upper body off the ground and reach your arms towards your legs. 3420.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold this position for a few seconds, then slowly lower your upper body and legs back down to the starting position. 3420.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3420.json#/instructions/4

Lift dumbbells in front of you to work the front of the shoulders.

Proposed staging
